# Pre-rank

Cheap desk scores from title + scout note only (no PDF, no repo) — an ordering for the audit queue, **not** a gate. Axes 1–5: fit = OS-course fit, feas = fits the machine, build = artifact completeness, room = incremental-add-on headroom. Papers with feas or build ≤ 2 are ranked below all others.

| # | total | fit | feas | build | room | paper | venue | topic | reason |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 20 | 5 | 5 | 5 | 5 | Baleen: ML Admission and Prefetching for Flash Caches (`fast24-baleen-ml-admission-and-prefetching-for`) | FAST 2024 | ml-for-systems | Flash-cache admission and prefetching evaluated entirely offline on released Meta traces; policy, residency model, and objective function are all swappable — an anchor-grade fit. |
| 2 | 20 | 5 | 5 | 5 | 5 | Kosmo: Efficient Online Miss Ratio Curve Generation for Eviction Policy Evaluation (`fast24-kosmo-efficient-online-miss-ratio-curve`) | FAST 2024 | caching | S3-FIFO-class target: trace-driven MRC generation for eviction policies, Rust artifact on Zenodo, public Twitter traces, no privilege needs, and adding policies or better sampling is a clean incremental hypothesis. |
| 3 | 20 | 5 | 5 | 5 | 5 | Demystifying and Improving Lazy Promotion in Cache Eviction (`vldb26-demystifying-and-improving-lazy-promotio`) | VLDB 2026 | caching | Archetypal cache-eviction study on libCacheSim traces from the cacheMon group: CPU-only, policy swaps are natural testable hypotheses. |
| 4 | 20 | 5 | 5 | 5 | 5 | SIEVE is Simpler than LRU: an Efficient Turn-Key Eviction Algorithm for Web Caches (`nsdi24-sieve-is-simpler-than-lru-an-efficient-t`) | NSDI 2024 | caching | CPU-only libCacheSim eviction study on 1559 open traces with 16-thread scalability claims that match this machine exactly; only disk for the full corpus needs trimming. |
